### 1. New module `packages/web/src/jds.ts` (pure functions, testable)
```ts
export const MAX_JDS = 8;
export const MIN_JD_CHARS = 40;
export const MAX_JD_CHARS = 6000;

export interface JdDraft { title: string; text: string; }

export function emptyJd(): JdDraft { return { title: '', text: '' }; }

/** 有效份数：整行留空（正文只有空白）不算一份 */
export function usableJds(jds: readonly JdDraft[]): { title: string; text: string }[] {
  return jds.filter((jd) => jd.text.trim().length > 0).map((jd) => ({ title: jd.title.trim(), text: jd.text }));
}

export function jdDraftProblem(jds: readonly JdDraft[]): string | null {
  const usable = jds.filter((jd) => jd.text.trim().length > 0);
  if (usable.length > MAX_JDS) return '最多 ' + MAX_JDS + ' 份 JD，现在有 ' + usable.length + ' 份。';
  for (let index = 0; index < jds.length; index += 1) {
    const text = jds[index].text.trim();
    if (text.length === 0) continue;
    const label = jds[index].title.trim() === '' ? '第 ' + (index+1) + ' 份' : '《' + jds[index].title.trim() + '》';
    if (text.length < MIN_JD_CHARS) return label + ' JD 太短了：至少 ' + MIN_JD_CHARS + ' 个字符，否则读不出要求。';
    if (text.length > MAX_JD_CHARS) return label + ' JD 太长了：最多 ' + MAX_JD_CHARS + ' 个字符。';
  }
  return null;
}

export function jdSummaryOf(jds: readonly JdDraft[]): string  // 用于界面提示「已填 N 份 · 共 M 字」
```
Hmm — server normalizes with trim per line? `normalizeJdText` in shared... jdProblems checks length on normalized text presumably. <details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Pane is `flex min-h-0 flex-col` with internal overflow — so stacking two Panes in a column needs each to have a bounded height. In a flex column with `min-h-0` and each pane `flex-1`? Pane has no flex-1 by default. If I put ClaimBoard and RequirementBoard in a `flex flex-col gap-3 min-h-0`, each Pane will size to content (flex-shrink default 1 with min-h-0 → they'll shrink but each body has overflow-auto). Better: give RequirementBoard `className="max-h-[42%]"`? Cleaner: make the middle column a flex column where ClaimBoard gets `flex-[3]` and RequirementBoard `flex-[2]` — Pane accepts className, and ClaimBoard passes `className`? ClaimBoard's Pane doesn't accept a className prop from outside. RequirementBoard does (props.className).

Simplest robust approach: middle column = `flex min-h-0 flex-col gap-3` containing:
- `<ClaimBoard ... />` (no flex → shrinks; but flex items with overflow-auto inside... Pane is a flex column with min-h-0; as a flex item in the parent column with `flex-shrink:1`, it will shrink to fit; its body will scroll. Two panes in a column: they'll each get their natural height, and if the total exceeds, they shrink proportionally (both have min-h-0 → can shrink to 0). That yields roughly equal splits, not great.

Explicit is better: wrap ClaimBoard in a `<div className="flex min-h-0 flex-[3] flex-col">` and RequirementBoard with `className='min-h-0 flex-[2]'`. But ClaimBoard's Pane is `flex min-h-0 flex-col` without h-full — inside a `flex flex-col` wrapper, the pane as a flex item stretches? Default align-items: stretch affects cross axis (width). Along the main axis (vertical), the pane's height is auto. Add `h-full`? With flex-[3] on a wrapper div and the pane inside needing to fill it: make wrapper `flex min-h-0 flex-[3] flex-col` and the Pane inside it — flex item in a column, needs `flex-1` to fill. Pane doesn't accept it... but wrapper could be `min-h-0 flex-[3] overflow-hidden` and Pane... Pane's height would be auto → content height, overflowing the wrapper (hidden). Not good.

Alternative simpler layout: put RequirementBoard in the *left* aside (below StageRail)? No, aside already full.

How about: middle column grid rows: `grid min-h-0 grid-rows-[minmax(0,3fr)_minmax(0,2fr)] gap-3`. Grid items stretch by default to fill their row! Pane is a flex column with min-h-0 and internal overflow — in a grid area it stretches to the row height, and its body scrolls. That's exactly right, and no need for className plumbing.

So middle column:
```tsx
        <div className="grid min-h-0 grid-rows-[minmax(0,3fr)_minmax(0,2fr)] gap-3">
          <ClaimBoard ... />
          <RequirementBoard ... />
        </div>
```
But on non-xl (stacked, `grid-cols-1` with overflow-auto on main), the min-h-0/grid-rows would squeeze badly. Currently main is `grid-cols-1 ... xl:grid-cols-[...] xl:overflow-hidden`; on small screens main scrolls and children are auto-height (single column, rows auto). With `grid-rows-[minmax(0,3fr)_minmax(0,2fr)]` always applied, on small screens the rows would be relative to... in an auto-height grid container, fr rows resolve to content height. Should be OK-ish. But to be safe, apply the row template only at xl: `grid min-h-0 gap-3 xl:grid-rows-[minmax(0,3fr)_minmax(0,2fr)]`. On non-xl, it's a single-column grid with auto rows — same as stacked. Good.

Right column: 
```tsx
        <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col gap-3">
          <div className="grid min-h-0 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-2">
            <ResumePane ... />
            <JdPane ... className="min-h-0" />
          </div>
          <RevisionBar ... />
        </div>
```
Wait, the outer right column is `flex flex-col`; the inner grid needs `min-h-0` and to take remaining space: give it `flex-1` → `grid min-h-0 flex-1 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-2`. But RevisionBar also has natural height. On non-xl, the whole main scrolls; `flex-1` is fine.

Hmm, but ResumePane and JdPane each are Panes in a 2-col grid → each stretched to the row height, internal scroll. Good.

Also widen the third column so two panes are usable: `xl:grid-cols-[330px_minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,660px)]`.

```
  /** 右栏的 JD 原文：跑过就用服务端那份（带编号），没跑过就用投料台里的草稿现算 */
  const shownJds = useMemo(

    [material.jds, draft.jds],
  );
  const activeJd = shownJds.find((jd) => jd.id === activeJdId) ?? shownJds[0] ?? null;
  /** 这一份 JD 上被抽成要求的位置：切分高亮用它 */
  const jdHits = useMemo(() => (activeJd === null ? [] : anchorsOfJd(sandbox, activeJd.id)), [sandbox, activeJd]);
  const requirementOf = useCallback(
    (id: string): Requirement | null => sandbox.requirements.find((item) => item.id === id) ?? null,
    [sandbox.requirements],
  );
  const claimTextOf = useCallback(
    (claimId: string): string | null => sandbox.claims.find((item) => item.claim.id === claimId)?.claim.text ?? null,
    [sandbox.claims],
  );
  const handleFocusJd = useCallback((anchor: Anchor | null) => dispatch({ type: 'focus-jd', anchor }), []);
  const handleFocusClaim = useCallback(
    (claimId: string) => {
      const found = sandbox.claims.find((item) => item.claim.id === claimId);
      if (found !== undefined) dispatch({ type: 'focus', anchor: found.claim.anchor });
    },
    [sandbox.claims],
  );
```

```js
const src = String.raw`/**
 * 岗位要求在前端状态里的折叠规则。
 *
 * 三件事值得守住：
 * ① 要求清单是**整批替换**，不是追加——它是「输入 + 抽出来的要求」派生的一张表，
 *    追加语义会让重跑一场之后旧要求留在界面上；
 * ② 只按 status 分组渲染，所以「哪条要求对应哪条主张」必须在状态层就能查到，
 *    不能靠组件自己二次遍历；
 * ③ 没有 JD 的场次必须能走到结论文案，不能显示成「0 条要求」这种像出错的字。
 */
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Requirement, RequirementMatch } from '../src/api.ts';
import {
  anchorsOfJd,
  applyEvent,
  initialSandbox,
  matchConclusionOf,
  requirementMatch,
  requirementsForClaim,
  sandboxReducer,
```

Hmm building nested JSON.stringify inside a JS expression string is messy. simpler: pass selector directly with data-shot in the expression using JSON.stringify for both parts:

```js
async function setShot(shot, text) {
  const expr = '(() => { const el = document.querySelector("[data-shot=\\"" + ' + JSON.stringify(shot) + ' + "\\"]"); if (el === null) return -1; const proto = el.tagName === "TEXTAREA" ? window.HTMLTextAreaElement.prototype : window.HTMLInputElement.prototype; const setter = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(proto, "value").set; setter.call(el, ' + JSON.stringify(text) + '); el.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true })); return el.value.length; })()';
  return evalJs(expr);
}
```
Wait, in the file content I need `[data-shot="` inside a single-quoted JS string: `'[data-shot=\"' + shot + '\"]'`. Let me write the helper as:
```js
async function setShot(name, text) {
  const expr = '(() => { const el = document.querySelector(\'[data-shot="\' + ' + JSON.stringify(name) + ' + \'"]\'); if (el === null) return -1; const proto = el.tagName === "TEXTAREA" ? window.HTMLTextAreaElement.prototype : window.HTMLInputElement.prototype; const setter = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(proto, "value").set; setter.call(el, ' + JSON.stringify(text) + '); el.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true })); return el.value.length; })()';
  return evalJs(expr);
}
```
That mirrors the existing style at :430 which uses `\'[data-shot="\' + name + \'"]\'`. Good.

Good. Now implement in scripted-demo.ts:

Add helper:
```ts
/** 从追问提示词里读出「岗位要求里对得上的几条」：每条的编号、对应主张、要求原文 */
function requirementNotesIn(prompt: string): { id: string; claimId: string; quote: string }[] {
  const out: { id: string; claimId: string; quote: string }[] = [];
  const re = /^- \[(R[0-9]+)\] [^\n（]*（对应主张 (C[0-9]+)，来自《[^》]*》）：[^\n]*\n\s+要求原文：([^\n]*)$/gm;
  let hit = re.exec(prompt);
  while (hit !== null) {
    out.push({ id: hit[1] ?? '', claimId: hit[2] ?? '', quote: hit[3] ?? '' });
    hit = re.exec(prompt);
  }
  return out;
}
```
Careful: the `label` contains '｜' and '（' — my regex `[^\n（]*（对应主张` requires no '（' before; label is e.g. '硬性要求｜答得含糊' ✓ (no full-width paren).

Then in probeText, build `const notes = requirementNotesIn(prompt);` and when pushing:
```ts
const note = notes.find((item) => item.claimId === block.claimId && !used.has(item.claimId));
if (note !== undefined) { used.add(note.claimId); probes.push({..., requirementId: note.id, jdQuote: note.quote}); }
else probes.push({...});
```


<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

The problem: `firstReq` is a Node-side variable but I embedded it inside the page expression string:

```js
'(() => { const b = document.querySelector("[data-shot=" + JSON.stringify("req-quote-" + firstReq.id) + "]"); ...'
```

The `+ JSON.stringify(...) +` is being evaluated in the *page* (inside the string), not in Node. Looking at existing patterns: existing code writes `'(function () { var chip = ... indexOf(' + JSON.stringify(name) + ') >= 0; ...'` — there the concatenation happens in Node because the string literal ends before `+`... Let me look: 

```js
const pickIndustry = (name) => '(function () { var chip = ... indexOf(' + JSON.stringify(name) + ') >= 0; ... })()';
```
Yes — here the quotes are split: the string literal ends with `indexOf(` then `+ JSON.stringify(name) +` (Node-side concat) then `') >= 0; ...'`. 

In my case I wrote `"...querySelector(\"[data-shot=\" + JSON.stringify(\"req-quote-\" + firstReq.id) + \"]\")..."` all inside a single-quoted Node string, but the inner `"` quotes opened/closed their own strings so the whole thing is a Node string containing a page expression that references firstReq. Then evalJs sent `... document.querySelector("[data-shot=" + JSON.stringify("req-quote-" + firstReq.id) + "]") ...` to the page where firstReq is undefined.

Also note: Node-side variables (like waitFor closures) worked elsewhere because they were inside arrow functions evaluated in Node.

Fix: build the expression in Node:
```js
const quoteBtn = 'req-quote-' + firstReq.id;
const quoteClicked = await evalJs('(() => { const b = document.querySelector("[data-shot=" + ' + JSON.stringify(quoteBtn) + ' + "]"); if (b === null) return "no-button"; b.click(); return "ok"; })()');
```
This is how the existing style does it (string literal terminated, `+ JSON.stringify(...) +`, resumed). Note the resulting page expression: `document.querySelector("[data-shot=" + "req-quote-R1" + "]")` → fine.

Let me fix that one line, plus resume e2e from scratch (it reruns everything). Also: the run reached line 475 (scenario B) and passed 5 checks — good.

Note: e2e left the browser open? The script threw before the finally block? No — the try/finally: the error happened inside try so finally should run (closeBrowser/cleanup). The `E2E=1` and traceback: Node crashed with the error thrown from evalJs inside the try → finally executed. Good.

Let me apply the fix.

</details>

I'll insert the new section between. Content (dense but valuable):

### 3.6.9 岗位要求审阅：1–8 份 JD，两处锚点

接第 3.6.8 节：模板解决「不知道怎么喂料」，这一节解决「手里有料但不知道够不够」。
用户可以贴 1–8 份 JD（`POST /api/sessions` 的 `jds`，每份 40–6000 字），服务端把它读成两类东西：

1. **要求清单**（`requirement` 表）。三段链路：`jd-extract` 逐份 JD **并发**抽要求（每份一次调用，上限 12 条），`jd-merge` 一次把所有 JD 的要求归并（同一件事写在两份 JD 里算一条，`sources` 记来源），`jd-match` 一次把全部要求和全部主张摆在一起逐条对照，给出 `答得上 / 答得含糊 / 简历里没写`。为什么归并和对照各只调一次：卡带按提示词指纹寻址，逐条调用会把 32 盘卡带变成上百盘，离线演示的成本与「提示词改一下就全部失效」的风险都会失控。
2. **追问的第二处锚点**。对「答得上」和「答得含糊」的要求，追问可以带一个 `requirementId` **同时**带一段 JD 原文引文（`jdQuote`）。要求原文 `resolveAnchor` 锚不回 JD 的，退回普通追问并在 rationale 里说明——宁可少一个锚，不编一个锚。

三个刻意的选择：

- **两处锚点不需要新机制**。`resolveAnchor(source, raw)` 从一开始就不关心 `source` 是简历还是 JD，
  所以「追问同时挂简历片段与 JD 片段」只是再调一次同一个函数；双锚点因此和单锚点一样可验证、可回放。
- **缺失的单独成栏，不生成追问**。「简历里没写」的要求只列出来，不派给面试官——面试官该追问的是简历里
  **说了但站不住**的话，把「简历没写」也变成质问会立刻退化成一份岗位说明书审阅清单，而不是压力测试。
- **空 JD 是合法的**。没有 JD 时整条链路跳过，面试官照各自的契约追问（`jdProblems([])` 返回空数组）。
  把「至少要有一份 JD」做成接口错误，会让纯简历场次整条路走不通——「还没上传」该不该提醒是界面的事，
  不是数据校验的事（F55）。

第三个真问题出在落库上：双锚点是 `Probe` 的**可选**字段，第一版只把引文写进事件流，业务表里没有列。
于是重开一场历史会话，双锚点就消失了——报告和界面都查不到「这条追问是照着哪条要求问的」。
修法是 schema v7 给 `probe` 补五列（`requirement_id` / `jd_quote` / `jd_start` / `jd_end` / `jd_exact`）
并做一次表重建迁移。**「可选字段」不等于「可以不落库」：只要它在界面上显示过，它就是这份记录的一部分**（F54）。

界面按「左简历右 JD」分屏：右栏在简历原文与岗位要求原文之间切换，中栏多一段「岗位要求对照」，
每条要求一张卡（要求正文、引文、为什么这么判、对应哪条主张），点引文右栏就聚焦到 JD 的那一段——
和简历引文用的是同一套聚焦机制。取舍见 ADR-0012、决策 D43。

65|五种判定不是设计出来的修辞，而是**证据审计的五个可能结论**：
66|
67|| 判定 | 含义 | 触发条件（产品语义） |
68|| --- | --- | --- |
69|| 站得住 | 主张有原文支撑，且追问下给出了可核验的口径 | 答得上 + 能援引原文 |
70|| 表述过度 | 主张本身没错，但说法超出了证据能支撑的范围 | 答得上但暴露了口径不一致 |
71|| 证据缺失 | 主张可能成立，但拿不出任何可定位到简历的证据 | 模型判定（责任在简历写法）；或模型判 solid/contradicts 却给不出引文 → 代码降级 |
72|| 与原文矛盾 | 追问下的回答与简历原文冲突 | 回答援引的原文与主张指向的事实相反 |
73|| 无法回答 | 候选人被追问后明确表示拿不出、不记得、没做过 | 模型判定（责任在人，风险最高的一类） |
74|

```text
127|---
128|
129|## 复现遇到问题怎么办
130|
131|| 现象 | 处理 |
132|| --- | --- |
133|| 双击 `启动.cmd` 窗口一闪就没 | 说明它中途失败了：在黑窗口里跑 `npm run launch` 能看到完整报错；最常见的是 Node 没装或版本低于 22.5 |
134|| 解压后双击没反应 / 报路径错误 | 目录路径里有中文或空格。挪到 `D:\crossexam` 这类纯英文路径再试 |
135|| 桌面没有出现「质询室 CrossExam」 | 双击 `装个快捷方式.cmd` 重试一次；它会打印建到了哪个路径，建失败会说原因 |
136|| 端口被占 | `CROSSEXAM_PORT=8899 npm run preview`（启动器同样认这个变量） |
137|| 想换数据库位置 / 清空重来 | `CROSSEXAM_DB=./data/other.db`；删掉 `data/` 就是全新状态 |
138|| 想换卡带目录 | `CROSSEXAM_FIXTURES=/path/to/llm` |
139|| 没装 Chrome/Edge | 只有 `npm run shots` 与 `npm run e2e:ui` 需要浏览器，其余命令都不需要 |
140|| 界面 404 / 空白 | 先跑 `npm run build`：服务端托管的是构建产物，不是源码 |
141|| 提示「回放模式找不到这一条记录」 | 说明提示词变了而卡带没更新：跑 `npm run seed:demo` 或用 `CROSSEXAM_MODE=record` 重录 |
142|

150|| **追问预算** | 每主张轮数 / 每人额度 / 全场总量都在代码里记账 | prompt 里写「最多问 3 次」模型
```
